# GB(4,4) complete classification — n = 24 (part 2 of 2)

Complete classification of the generalized bicycle codes GB(4,4) of length `n` = 5 to 25: for each parameter `[[N, k, d]]` (`N = 2n`), every pair `(A, B)` of polynomials found by the search (see `../algorithms/gb44_all_representatives.m`).

Each length `n` lists its best parameters: for each dimension `k`, the parameters of greatest distance `d`.

The length n = 24 is split into two files by parameter: this file holds parameters [[48, 2, 9]] to [[48, 36, 2]]; the beginning is in [gb44_complete_classification_n24_part1.md](gb44_complete_classification_n24_part1.md).

<details>
<summary><b>n = 24</b> — best parameters: [[48, 2, 10]], [[48, 4, 8]], [[48, 6, 8]], [[48, 8, 6]], [[48, 10, 6]], [[48, 12, 4]], [[48, 14, 4]], [[48, 16, 4]], [[48, 18, 4]], [[48, 20, 3]], [[48, 24, 2]], [[48, 26, 2]], [[48, 28, 2]], [[48, 30, 2]], [[48, 32, 2]], [[48, 36, 2]]</summary>

<details>
